# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 605–634

## Page 668 — IIIF Canvas #605

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0605.JP2` (2400 × 2990 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0605.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0605.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation of the electuary for rising of the womb, followed by herbal remedies with parsnip, columbine, and peony, three recipes for precious womb waters (including distilled extracts), and two recipes for womb pills.

## Page 669 — IIIF Canvas #606

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0606.JP2` (2401 × 2689 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0606.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0606.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Remedies for rising of the womb (stinking chamomile, foot-bath, warnings for pregnant women, mugwort extract) and a section on uterine apostemes/abscesses (Von Apostem der ☿v), including an illustrated womb syringe and clyster/douching recipes for maturing, cleansing, and healing.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Brass/gold clyster syringe and angled nozzle** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Watercolor illustration in yellow-ochre/gold and grey shading in the right column depicting a cylindrical womb-douching syringe with ribbed grip and nozzle, alongside a small L-shaped angled nozzle or key to the right.

## Page 670 — IIIF Canvas #607

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0607.JP2` (2401 × 3002 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0607.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0607.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Recto folio (p. 670, pencil 301) with two columns framed in red-ink borders and a red-ink column divider. Left column continues a uterine/pain treatment and gives recipes for soothing salves, clysters, and mucilaginous ointments for the womb (♀V). Right column opens with a rubricated drying salve in red ink, followed by a horizontal red-ink rule and a major rubricated heading on prolapse/softening of the uterus (♀V), discussing causes, patient compliance, and initial purging, diet, and fumigation remedies.

### Left Column

| :---: | :--- | :--- |

## Page 671 — IIIF Canvas #608

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0608.JP2` (2401 × 2689 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0608.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0608.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Verso folio (p. 671) framed in red-ink borders with a red-ink column divider. Left column describes positioning the patient, applying pessaries ('Job äpfel'), and decoctions for uterine swelling and heat. Right column gives a plaster for the navel, a lodestone plaster for uterine prolapse, a chemical oil ('Oleum für die beich') with alchemical symbols, and instructions with a watercolor illustration of a uterine pessary ball ('♀V. khugel oder chränzel') with its cord.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Wie die ♀V. khugel oder chränzel sollen gemacht.** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Watercolor illustration in the lower right column depicting a yellow-ochre spherical uterine pessary (ball) with a dark grey opening at the top containing a knotted cord and a twisted grey-blue string hanging from the bottom onto a pale green ground strip.

## Page 672 — IIIF Canvas #609

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0609.JP2` (2401 × 2993 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0609.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0609.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Recto folio (p. 672, pencil 302) framed in red-ink borders with a red-ink column divider and a red-ink boxed page number. Left column gives detailed instructions for making and waxing cork uterine pessaries ('khränzel'/'khugel'), sizing them, and warning of cancer if left untreated. Right column gives a balm for the loins, notes common steaming/washing decoctions, and after a horizontal red-ink divider rule begins a new section on other uterine ailments ('Von Andern Zue sendten Und khranckheiten der ♀V'), starting with chilling and phlegm.

## Page 674 — IIIF Canvas #613

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0613.JP2` (2401 × 3008 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0613.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0613.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Obstetric remedies and plasters to prevent miscarriage and strengthen the unborn child in the womb, including dietary/purging advice, internal powders (tormentil, pearls, Armenian bole, scarlet), a rubricated strengthening plaster, and advice on bloodletting in cases of excess blood.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0614.JP2` (2401 × 2689 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0614.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0614.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

## Page 676 — IIIF Canvas #615

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0615.JP2` (2400 × 2992 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0615.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0615.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Remedies for difficult labor, fainting from foul vapors, expelling a dead fetus and afterbirth (white amber, saffron, cassia, borax, white onion, lily water, storax, castoreum), remedies for the poor (gum ammoniac, sabina, galbanum, rue), and a new section on 'When Impurity is Retained in Women in Childbed', all preceded by alchemical/astrological cipher lines.

## Page 677 — IIIF Canvas #616

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0616.JP2` (2401 × 2689 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0616.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0616.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

## Page 679 — IIIF Canvas #618

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0618.JP2` (2401 × 2689 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0618.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0618.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation of remedies for hardened/obstructed milk in nursing mothers (softening and dispersing plasters with fenugreek, bread crumbs, marshmallow, mint, and whey), followed by a section on breast apostemes (abscesses) and ulcers, distinguishing causes by the four humors (blood, bile, phlegm).

Remedies for breast lesions, abscesses, and pain, followed by a red watercolor illustration of a crayfish/crab and a rubricated section on cancer (Krebs) of the breasts, including prognosis, surgical excision limits, and a palliative poultice/ointment for hidden cancer.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0625.JP2` (2401 × 3027 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0625.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0625.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Medical recipes and remedies for pregnant women: treating dizziness (Schwindel), fainting spells (Omachten), dangerous vomiting/coughing (Gießen ist gefärlich, with rubricated heading in red ink), and side stitches/pleurisy (Beiden Stöchen).

## Page 690. — IIIF Canvas #627

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0627.JP2` (2400 × 3042 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0627.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0627.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation of remedies for strange cravings in pregnancy (including a distilled Malvasia/citron peel water), constipation in pregnant women (Von Verstopffung des Leibes der Schwangern Frauen) with a purgative senna-jujube decoction, and diarrhea in pregnant women (Durch Bruch der Schwangern Frauen).

## Page 691 — IIIF Canvas #628

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0628.JP2` (2401 × 2689 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0628.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0628.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Gynecological and obstetric recipes in two columns: remedies for cramps in pregnant women, swelling of the feet in pregnancy, powder against miscarriage (rubricated), remedies for fluor albus (whites, rubricated), baths and internal powders for uterine complaints, and remedies for menstruation during pregnancy (rubricated).

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0629.JP2` (2400 × 3017 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0629.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0629.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

A multi-part gynecological regimen ('Für die Rainigung der ♀', rubricated with crown symbol) comprising purging potions (A), dissolving powders (B), strengthening powders (C), bath minerals (E) and herb sachets (F), a 20-day schedule of administration, bath preparation instructions, and remedies with cryptic alchemical symbols (rubricated).

## Page 697 — IIIF Canvas #634

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0634.JP2` (2400 × 2721 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0634.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0634.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Page 697 (verso) continues a mercurial ointment recipe at the top of the left column, followed by remedies for Morbus Gallicus (French disease) including a fumigation, drinks for French-disease ulcers and phlegm obstruction, a cleansing drink with a red-ink note on dosage, and a remedy for rebellious French disease using gold. The right column opens with a watercolor illustration of a patient in bed, followed by a rubricated treatise heading 'Khurzer anfang: Gewiser vnd geradter Weg der Salifacion Wider Morbum Galicum' detailing purgatives, Turpethum minerale, dietary regimen, and decoctions.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Patient lying in bed (Salivation cure for Morbus Gallicus)** (`#xywh=400,400,500,1000`): Watercolor illustration at the top of the right column depicting a pale patient in a white nightshirt and cap lying in a wooden bedstead under a rose-pink blanket, resting against white pillows, with small green trees and distant landscape elements on the left and right.
